FILE: apps\web\components\ui\image-cursor-trail.tsx
-----------------------------------------------------------------
"use client";

/* eslint-disable @next/next/no-img-element -- intentional: this file renders raw <img> for effects (cursor trails / animated corridors) where next/image isn't a fit. */

import React, { createRef, useEffect, useRef, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

interface ImageMouseTrailProps {
  items: string[];
  children?: ReactNode;
  className?: string;
  imgClass?: string;
  distance?: number;
  maxNumberOfImages?: number;
  fadeAnimation?: boolean;
  /** When false, the component stops tracking/showing images (used for hover-triggered galleries). */
  active?: boolean;
}

export function ImageCursorTrail({
  items,
  children,
  className,
  maxNumberOfImages = 5,
  imgClass = "w-40 h-48",
  distance = 20,
  fadeAnimation = false,
  active = true,
}: ImageMouseTrailProps) {
  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const refs = useRef(items.map(() => createRef<HTMLImageElement>()));
  const currentZIndexRef = useRef(1);
  const globalIndexRef = useRef(0);
  const lastRef = useRef({ x: 0, y: 0 });

  const activate = (image: HTMLImageElement, x: number, y: number) => {
    const containerRect = containerRef.current?.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (!containerRect) return;
    const relativeX = x - containerRect.left;
    const relativeY = y - containerRect.top;
    image.style.left = `${relativeX}px`;
    image.style.top = `${relativeY}px`;

    if (currentZIndexRef.current > 40) {
      currentZIndexRef.current = 1;
    }
    image.style.zIndex = String(currentZIndexRef.current);
    currentZIndexRef.current++;

    image.dataset.status = "active";
    if (fadeAnimation) {
      setTimeout(() => {
        image.dataset.status = "inactive";
      }, 1500);
    }
    lastRef.current = { x, y };
  };

  const deactivate = (image: HTMLImageElement) => {
    image.dataset.status = "inactive";
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!active) {
      refs.current.forEach((r) => {
        if (r.current) r.current.dataset.status = "inactive";
      });
      return;
    }

    function handlePoint(clientX: number, clientY: number) {
      const rect = containerRef.current?.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (!rect) return;
      const inside =
        clientX >= rect.left &&
        clientX <= rect.right &&
        clientY >= rect.top &&
        clientY <= rect.bottom;
      if (!inside) return;

      const distFromLast = Math.hypot(clientX - lastRef.current.x, clientY - lastRef.current.y);
      if (distFromLast > window.innerWidth / distance) {
        const lead = refs.current[globalIndexRef.current % refs.current.length].current;
        const tail =
          refs.current[
            (globalIndexRef.current - maxNumberOfImages) % refs.current.length
          ]?.current;
        if (lead) activate(lead, clientX, clientY);
        if (tail) deactivate(tail);
        globalIndexRef.current++;
      }
    }

    function onMouseMove(e: MouseEvent) {
      handlePoint(e.clientX, e.clientY);
    }
    function onTouchMove(e: TouchEvent) {
      const t = e.touches[0];
      if (t) handlePoint(t.clientX, t.clientY);
    }

    window.addEventListener("mousemove", onMouseMove);
    window.addEventListener("touchmove", onTouchMove);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("mousemove", onMouseMove);
      window.removeEventListener("touchmove", onTouchMove);
    };
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [active, distance, maxNumberOfImages, fadeAnimation]);

  return (
    <section
      ref={containerRef}
      className={cn(
        "relative grid h-[600px] w-full place-content-center overflow-hidden rounded-lg pointer-events-none",
        className
      )}
    >
      {items.map((item, index) => (
        <img
          key={index}
          className={cn(
            "opacity:0 data-[status='active']:ease-out-expo pointer-events-none absolute -translate-x-[50%] -translate-y-[50%] scale-0 rounded-3xl object-cover transition-transform duration-300 data-[status='active']:scale-100 data-[status='active']:opacity-100 data-[status='active']:duration-500",
            imgClass
          )}
          data-index={index}
          data-status="inactive"
          src={item}
          alt={`saachi-ai-showcase-${index}`}
          ref={refs.current[index]}
        />
      ))}
      {children}
    </section>
  );
}

export default ImageCursorTrail;

FILE: apps\web\components\ui\image-stream-hero.tsx
-----------------------------------------------------------------
"use client";

/* eslint-disable @next/next/no-img-element -- intentional: this file renders raw <img> for effects (cursor trails / animated corridors) where next/image isn't a fit. */

import * as React from "react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

export type CorridorPath = {
  perspective?: number;
  cardWidth?: number;
  cardHeight?: number;
  cardRadius?: number;
  birthHeight?: number;
  exitHeight?: number;
  railBirth?: number;
  railExit?: number;
  fan?: number;
  turnBirth?: number;
  turnExit?: number;
  stops?: number;
};

const PATH: Required<CorridorPath> = {
  perspective: 30,
  cardWidth: 18,
  cardHeight: 25,
  cardRadius: 0.4,
  birthHeight: 2.6,
  exitHeight: 46,
  railBirth: -11,
  railExit: 44,
  fan: 3.3,
  turnBirth: 6,
  turnExit: 28,
  stops: 24
};

function keyframes(dir: 1 | -1, name: string, p: Required<CorridorPath>) {
  const steps: string[] = [];
  for (let s = 0; s <= p.stops; s++) {
    const u = s / p.stops;
    const scale = (p.birthHeight / p.cardHeight) * Math.pow(p.exitHeight / p.birthHeight, u);
    const z = p.perspective * (1 - 1 / scale);
    const rail = p.railExit - (p.railExit - p.railBirth) * Math.pow(1 - u, p.fan);
    const turn = p.turnBirth + (p.turnExit - p.turnBirth) * u;
    steps.push(
      `${(u * 100).toFixed(2)}%{transform:translate3d(${(dir * rail).toFixed(2)}cqw,0,${z.toFixed(2)}cqw) rotateY(${(-dir * turn).toFixed(2)}deg)}`
    );
  }
  return `@keyframes ${name}{${steps.join("")}}`;
}

export type StreamImage = { src: string; alt?: string };

export type ImageStreamHeroProps = {
  images: StreamImage[];
  cards?: number;
  speed?: number;
  axis?: number;
  path?: CorridorPath;
  children?: React.ReactNode;
  className?: string;
};

export function ImageStreamHero({ images, cards = 9, speed = 18, axis = 55, path, children, className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"div"> & ImageStreamHeroProps) {
  const id = React.useId().replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, "");
  const right = `ish-r-${id}`;
  const left = `ish-l-${id}`;
  const card = `ish-c-${id}`;

  const p = React.useMemo(() => ({ ...PATH, ...path }), [path]);

  const css = React.useMemo(
    () =>
      `${keyframes(1, right, p)}${keyframes(-1, left, p)}` +
      `@media(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){.${card}{animation-play-state:paused}}`,
    [right, left, card, p]
  );

  return (
    <div className={cn("relative overflow-hidden", className)} {...props} style={{ containerType: "inline-size", ...props.style }}>
      <style>{css}</style>

      <div aria-hidden className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0" style={{ perspective: `${p.perspective}cqw`, perspectiveOrigin: `50% ${axis}%` }}>
        <div className="absolute inset-0" style={{ transformStyle: "preserve-3d" }}>
          {[right, left].map((name) =>
            Array.from({ length: cards }, (_, i) => {
              const img = images[i % Math.max(images.length, 1)];
              return (
                <div
                  key={`${name}-${i}`}
                  className={cn(card, "absolute overflow-hidden")}
                  style={{
                    left: "50%",
                    top: `${axis}%`,
                    width: `${p.cardWidth}cqw`,
                    height: `${p.cardHeight}cqw`,
                    marginLeft: `${-p.cardWidth / 2}cqw`,
                    marginTop: `${-p.cardHeight / 2}cqw`,
                    borderRadius: `${p.cardRadius}cqw`,
                    animation: `${name} ${speed}s linear infinite`,
                    animationDelay: `${-(i * speed) / cards}s`,
                    backfaceVisibility: "hidden"
                  }}
                >
                  {img ? (
                    <img src={img.src} alt={img.alt ?? ""} loading="lazy" decoding="async" className="h-full w-full object-cover" draggable={false} />
                  ) : null}
                </div>
              );
            })
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {children}
    </div>
  );
}

export default ImageStreamHero;

"use client";

import React, { useState, useMemo } from "react";
import { motion, AnimatePresence, LayoutGroup } from "motion/react";
import { HugeiconsIcon } from "@hugeicons/react";
import {
  DashboardSquare01Icon,
  BookOpen01Icon,
  UserGroupIcon,
  StarIcon,
  Add01Icon,
  CircleArrowUpRight02Icon,
  Search01Icon,
  BarChartIcon,
  Tick01Icon,
  Settings02Icon,
  InformationCircleIcon,
  Wallet01Icon,
  UserIcon,
} from "@hugeicons/core-free-icons";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

interface TabConfig {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  icon: any;
  badge?: string;
  header: string;
  description: string;
}

const TABS: TabConfig[] = [
  {
    id: "overview",
    label: "Overview",
    icon: DashboardSquare01Icon,
    header: "Course Performance",
    description: "Daily summary across all your courses.",
  },
  {
    id: "courses",
    label: "Courses",
    icon: BookOpen01Icon,
    header: "My Courses",
    description: "Track status and enrollment per course.",
    badge: "4",
  },
  {
    id: "students",
    label: "Students",
    icon: UserGroupIcon,
    header: "Student Activity",
    description: "Recent learners across your courses.",
    badge: "654",
  },
  {
    id: "reviews",
    label: "Reviews",
    icon: StarIcon,
    header: "Recent Reviews",
    description: "What learners are saying right now.",
    badge: "3",
  },
];

export function InstructorBentoCard() {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState(TABS[0]);

  const content = useMemo(() => {
    switch (activeTab.id) {
      case "overview":
        return <OverviewPanel />;
      case "courses":
        return <CoursesPanel />;
      case "students":
        return <StudentsPanel />;
      case "reviews":
        return <ReviewsPanel />;
      default:
        return null;
    }
  }, [activeTab.id]);

  return (
    <div className="flex items-center justify-center w-full antialiased">
      <div className="group relative w-full max-w-xl overflow-hidden rounded-3xl sm:rounded-4xl border border-line bg-card shadow-2xl shadow-accent-violet/5 transition-all duration-500 hover:shadow-accent-violet/10 hover:-translate-y-1 m-0">
        <div className="p-4 sm:p-6 space-y-1.5 z-10 relative">
          <h2 className="text-xs text-ink-soft uppercase">
            Instructor Dashboard
          </h2>
          <p className="text-lg sm:text-2xl text-ink font-display font-medium leading-snug max-w-[480px]">
            Course performance and student activity in one place.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="relative w-full h-[260px] sm:h-[300px] overflow-hidden rounded-2xl sm:rounded-[2rem]">
          <div className="absolute top-16 left-16 w-full h-full bg-paper-dim rounded-3xl border border-line/50 opacity-80" />

          <div className="absolute top-8 left-24 w-full h-full bg-card rounded-tl-3xl shadow-xl flex flex-col overflow-hidden ring-6 ring-line">
            <div className="px-5 py-4 rounded-tl-3xl border-b border-line/70 flex items-center relative backdrop-blur-sm">
              <div className="flex gap-1.5">
                <div className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-ink-soft/20" />
                <div className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-ink-soft/20" />
                <div className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-ink-soft/20" />
              </div>
              <div className="absolute left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="text-xs text-ink-soft/50 uppercase">
                  Saachi AI Instructor
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-1 overflow-hidden">
              <div className="w-36 border-r border-line/30 p-2 flex flex-col gap-1 pt-6 bg-paper-dim/20">
                <LayoutGroup>
                  {TABS.map((tab) => {
                    const isActive = activeTab.id === tab.id;
                    const Icon = tab.icon;

                    return (
                      <button
                        key={tab.id}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab)}
                        className={cn(
                          "relative flex items-center gap-1.5 p-2 rounded-xl text-xs transition-colors cursor-pointer",
                          isActive
                            ? "text-ink"
                            : "text-ink-soft hover:text-ink",
                        )}
                      >
                        <HugeiconsIcon
                          icon={Icon}
                          size={14}
                          className="z-20 shrink-0 relative"
                        />
                        <span className="truncate z-20 relative font-medium">
                          {tab.label}
                        </span>
                        {tab.badge && (
                          <span
                            className={cn(
                              "ml-auto text-[8px] leading-none py-0.5 px-1 rounded-md tabular-nums transition-all z-20 relative",
                              isActive
                                ? "bg-accent-violet/10 text-accent-violet border border-accent-violet/20"
                                : "bg-paper-dim text-ink-soft border border-transparent",
                            )}
                          >
                            {tab.badge}
                          </span>
                        )}

                        {isActive && (
                          <motion.div
                            layoutId="instructor-sidebar-pill"
                            className="absolute left-0 w-[2px] h-4 rounded-full bg-accent-violet z-30 border border-accent-violet/20"
                            transition={{ type: "spring", bounce: 0.2, duration: 0.6 }}
                          />
                        )}
                        {isActive && (
                          <motion.div
                            layoutId="instructor-background-indicator"
                            className="absolute inset-0 rounded-lg bg-paper-dim border border-line/40"
                            transition={{ type: "spring", bounce: 0.2, duration: 0.6 }}
                          />
                        )}
                      </button>
                    );
                  })}
                </LayoutGroup>
              </div>

              <div className="flex-1 bg-card p-5 pt-6 flex flex-col gap-4 overflow-hidden relative">
                <header className="flex flex-col gap-0.5">
                  <h3 className="text-xs font-semibold text-ink tracking-tight line-clamp-1 uppercase opacity-60">
                    {activeTab.header}
                  </h3>
                  <p className="text-[10px] text-ink-soft font-normal leading-tight line-clamp-1">
                    {activeTab.description}
                  </p>
                </header>

                <AnimatePresence mode="popLayout" initial={false}>
                  <motion.div
                    key={activeTab.id}
                    initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8, filter: "blur(4px)" }}
                    animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0, filter: "blur(0px)" }}
                    exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -8, filter: "blur(4px)" }}
                    transition={{ duration: 0.3, ease: [0.23, 1, 0.32, 1] }}
                    className="flex-1"
                  >
                    {content}
                  </motion.div>
                </AnimatePresence>

                <div className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 h-10 bg-linear-to-t from-card to-transparent pointer-events-none z-20" />
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default InstructorBentoCard;

FILE: apps\web\components\ui\instructor-onboarding-checklist.tsx
-----------------------------------------------------------------
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { ChevronDown, X, CircleCheck, Circle } from "lucide-react";
import { useInstructorCourseStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { Button, cn } from "@saachi/ui";

const DISMISS_KEY = "saachi-instructor-onboarding-dismissed";

type ChecklistItem = {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  description: string;
  href: string;
  done: boolean;
};

export function InstructorOnboardingChecklist() {
  const courses = useInstructorCourseStore((s) => s.courses);

  const [dismissed, setDismissed] = useState(true); // default true until we check localStorage (avoids flash)
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState(true);
  const [hydrated, setHydrated] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    setHydrated(true);
    const stored = window.localStorage.getItem(DISMISS_KEY);
    setDismissed(stored === "true");
  }, []);

  // NOTE: demo-only heuristics. Replace with real profile/payout/mentorship
  // status flags from the backend once available.
  const hasPublishedCourse = courses.some((c) => c.status === "published");
  const hasAnyCourse = courses.length > 0;

  const items: ChecklistItem[] = [
    {
      id: "profile",
      label: "Complete your profile",
      description: "Add a bio and portfolio link so learners know who's teaching.",
      href: "/instructor/settings",
      done: true // NOTE: demo-only, assume complete since session always has a name
    },
    {
      id: "payout",
      label: "Add a payout method",
      description: "Connect a bank or wallet account so you can withdraw earnings.",
      href: "/instructor/payouts/methods",
      done: false
    },
    {
      id: "publish",
      label: "Publish your first course",
      description: "Get your course live and start accepting enrollments.",
      href: "/instructor/courses",
      done: hasPublishedCourse
    },
    {
      id: "mentorship",
      label: "Enable mentorship",
      description: "Offer paid 1-on-1 sessions to learners who finish your courses.",
      href: "/instructor/mentorship",
      done: false
    }
  ];

  const completedCount = items.filter((i) => i.done).length;
  const allDone = completedCount === items.length;

  function handleDismiss() {
    window.localStorage.setItem(DISMISS_KEY, "true");
    setDismissed(true);
  }

  if (!hydrated || dismissed || !hasAnyCourse === false) {
    // still render normally once hydrated; this branch only prevents SSR flash
  }
  if (!hydrated) return null;
  if (dismissed) return null;

  return (
    <div className="mb-10 overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 px-5 py-4">
        <div className="flex-1">
          <div className="mb-1.5 flex items-center gap-2">
            <h2 className="text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">Get set up on Saachi AI</h2>
            <span className="rounded-full bg-paper-dim px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-semibold text-ink-soft">
              {completedCount} of {items.length} complete
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="h-1.5 w-full max-w-xs overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-paper-dim">
            <div
              className="h-full rounded-full bg-gold transition-all duration-300 ease-out"
              style={{ width: `${(completedCount / items.length) * 100}%` }}
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setExpanded((v) => !v)}
          aria-label={expanded ? "Collapse checklist" : "Expand checklist"}
          className="flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink"
        >
          <ChevronDown
            size={16}
            className="transition-transform duration-200 ease-out"
            style={{ transform: expanded ? "rotate(180deg)" : "rotate(0deg)" }}
          />
        </button>

        {allDone && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleDismiss}
            aria-label="Dismiss checklist"
            className="flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink"
          >
            <X size={16} />
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      {expanded && (
        <div className="border-t border-line px-5 py-2">
          {items.map((item) => (
            <div
              key={item.id}
              className="flex items-center gap-3 border-b border-line py-3 last:border-0"
            >
              <span className={cn("shrink-0", item.done ? "text-teal" : "text-ink-soft/40")}>
                {item.done ? <CircleCheck size={18} /> : <Circle size={18} />}
              </span>
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <p
                  className={cn(
                    "text-[13.5px] font-semibold",
                    item.done ? "text-ink-soft line-through" : "text-ink"
                  )}
                >
                  {item.label}
                </p>
                <p className="text-[12px] text-ink-soft">{item.description}</p>
              </div>
              {!item.done && (
                <Link href={item.href} className="shrink-0">
                  <Button size="sm" variant="secondary">
                    Go
                  </Button>
                </Link>
              )}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export default InstructorOnboardingChecklist;

FILE: apps\web\components\ui\instructor-search-bar.tsx
-----------------------------------------------------------------
"use client";

import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { Search, BookOpen, Users, Star, ArrowUpRight } from "lucide-react";
import { useInstructorCourseStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

type ResultItem = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  sub?: string;
  href: string;
};

type ResultGroup = {
  key: string;
  label: string;
  icon: React.ReactNode;
  items: ResultItem[];
};

// NOTE: demo-only dummy data for students/reviews. Courses come from the
// real instructor course store. Replace students/reviews with
// useInstructorStudents() / useInstructorReviews() once confirmed.
const DUMMY_STUDENTS = [
  { id: "stu_1", name: "Hina Malik", courseTitle: "Automate Everything with n8n & AI" },
  { id: "stu_2", name: "Omar Sheikh", courseTitle: "Automate Everything with n8n & AI" },
  { id: "stu_3", name: "Sara Iqbal", courseTitle: "AI Chatbots for WhatsApp Support" },
  { id: "stu_4", name: "Noor Fatima", courseTitle: "Automate Everything with n8n & AI" },
  { id: "stu_5", name: "Ali Raza", courseTitle: "AI Chatbots for WhatsApp Support" },
  { id: "stu_6", name: "Danish Iqbal", courseTitle: "Prompt Engineering for Client Work" }
];

const DUMMY_REVIEWS = [
  { id: "rev_i1", author: "Bilal S.", courseTitle: "Automate Everything with n8n & AI", comment: "Very practical, loved it." },
  { id: "rev_i2", author: "Fatima R.", courseTitle: "AI Chatbots for WhatsApp Support", comment: "Good pacing, clear examples." },
  { id: "rev_i3", author: "Omar Sheikh", courseTitle: "Automate Everything with n8n & AI", comment: "Best automation course I've taken." }
];

export function InstructorSearchBar() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const courses = useInstructorCourseStore((s) => s.courses);

  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const wrapperRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    function handleClickOutside(e: MouseEvent) {
      if (wrapperRef.current && !wrapperRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
        setOpen(false);
      }
    }
    document.addEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("mousedown", handleClickOutside);
  }, []);

  const groups: ResultGroup[] = useMemo(() => {
    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!q) return [];

    const courseResults: ResultItem[] = courses
      .filter((c) => c.title.toLowerCase().includes(q))
      .slice(0, 5)
      .map((c) => ({
        id: c.id,
        title: c.title,
        sub: c.status === "published" ? "Published" : c.status === "pending_review" ? "Pending review" : "Draft",
        href: `/instructor/courses/${c.id}/edit`
      }));

    const studentResults: ResultItem[] = DUMMY_STUDENTS
      .filter((s) => s.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || s.courseTitle.toLowerCase().includes(q))
      .slice(0, 5)
      .map((s) => ({
        id: s.id,
        title: s.name,
        sub: s.courseTitle,
        href: "/instructor/students"
      }));

    const reviewResults: ResultItem[] = DUMMY_REVIEWS
      .filter(
        (r) =>
          r.author.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
          r.courseTitle.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
          r.comment.toLowerCase().includes(q)
      )
      .slice(0, 5)
      .map((r) => ({
        id: r.id,
        title: `${r.author} - ${r.courseTitle}`,
        sub: r.comment,
        href: "/instructor/reviews"
      }));

    const out: ResultGroup[] = [];
    if (courseResults.length > 0) out.push({ key: "courses", label: "Courses", icon: <BookOpen size={12} />, items: courseResults });
    if (studentResults.length > 0) out.push({ key: "students", label: "Students", icon: <Users size={12} />, items: studentResults });
    if (reviewResults.length > 0) out.push({ key: "reviews", label: "Reviews", icon: <Star size={12} />, items: reviewResults });
    return out;
  }, [courses, query]);

  const totalResults = groups.reduce((sum, g) => sum + g.items.length, 0);
  const showDropdown = open && query.trim().length > 0;

  function handleSelect(href: string) {
    setOpen(false);
    setQuery("");
    router.push(href);
  }

  return (
    <div ref={wrapperRef} className="relative hidden md:block">
      <div className="flex h-9 w-64 items-center gap-2 rounded-control border border-line bg-paper-dim px-3">
        <Search size={14} className="shrink-0 text-ink-soft" />
        <input
          value={query}
          onChange={(e) => {
            setQuery(e.target.value);
            setOpen(true);
          }}
          onFocus={() => setOpen(true)}
          placeholder="Search your courses, students..."
          className="w-full bg-transparent text-[12.5px] text-ink placeholder:text-ink-soft/60 outline-none"
        />
      </div>

      {showDropdown && (
        <div className="absolute right-0 top-[calc(100%+8px)] z-50 w-[340px] overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card shadow-xl">
          {totalResults === 0 ? (
            <div className="px-4 py-8 text-center text-[12.5px] text-ink-soft">
              No results for &quot;{query}&quot;
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="max-h-[380px] overflow-y-auto p-1.5">
              {groups.map((group) => (
                <div key={group.key} className="mb-1 last:mb-0">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 pb-1 pt-2 text-[10.5px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.06em] text-ink-soft/60">
                    {group.icon}
                    {group.label}
                  </div>
                  {group.items.map((item) => (
                    <button
                      key={item.id}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleSelect(item.href)}
                      className="flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-control px-2.5 py-2 text-left transition-colors duration-100 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim"
                    >
                      <div className="min-w-0">
                        <p className="truncate text-[12.5px] font-medium text-ink">{item.title}</p>
                        {item.sub && <p className="truncate text-[11px] text-ink-soft">{item.sub}</p>}
                      </div>
                      <ArrowUpRight size={12} className="shrink-0 text-ink-soft/50" />
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export default InstructorSearchBar;

/* --------------------------------- Icon rail ------------------------------ */

function resolveGroupHref(group: InstructorNavGroup): string {
  const firstItem = group.items[0];
  if (!firstItem) return "/instructor/dashboard";
  return firstItem.href.split("?")[0];
}

export function InstructorIconRail({
  activeGroupId,
  onSelect
}: {
  activeGroupId: string;
  onSelect: (id: string) => void;
}) {
  const router = useRouter();

  function handleClick(group: InstructorNavGroup) {
    onSelect(group.id);
    router.push(resolveGroupHref(group));
  }

  return (
    <div className="hidden md:flex w-16 shrink-0 flex-col items-center gap-2 border-r border-line bg-card py-4">
      {INSTRUCTOR_SIDEBAR.map((group) => {
        const Icon = group.icon;
        const isActive = group.id === activeGroupId;
        return (
          <button
            key={group.id}
            type="button"
            title={group.label}
            onClick={() => handleClick(group)}
            className={cn(
              "relative flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-control transition-colors duration-200 ease-out",
              isActive ? "bg-gold text-ink" : "text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink"
            )}
            style={{ transitionTimingFunction: softSpring }}
          >
            <Icon size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} />
            {group.badge && (
              <span className="absolute -right-1 -top-1 flex h-4 min-w-4 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-gold-dark px-1 text-[9px] font-semibold text-white">
                {group.badge}
              </span>
            )}
          </button>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ------------------------------- Detail panel ----------------------------- */

export function InstructorDetailPanel({
  activeGroupId,
  onNavigate,
  showBrand = false,
  collapsed: collapsedProp,
  onCollapsedChange
}: {
  activeGroupId: string;
  onNavigate?: () => void;
  showBrand?: boolean;
  /** Optional controlled collapse state. If omitted, the panel manages its own state. */
  collapsed?: boolean;
  onCollapsedChange?: (collapsed: boolean) => void;
}) {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const [internalCollapsed, setInternalCollapsed] = useState(false);
  const collapsed = collapsedProp ?? internalCollapsed;
  const setCollapsed = (value: boolean | ((prev: boolean) => boolean)) => {
    const next = typeof value === "function" ? (value as (prev: boolean) => boolean)(collapsed) : value;
    if (onCollapsedChange) onCollapsedChange(next);
    else setInternalCollapsed(next);
  };
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");

  const group = INSTRUCTOR_SIDEBAR.find((g) => g.id === activeGroupId) ?? INSTRUCTOR_SIDEBAR[0];

  const toggle = (key: string) => {
    setExpanded((prev) => {
      const next = new Set(prev);
      if (next.has(key)) next.delete(key);
      else next.add(key);
      return next;
    });
  };

  const filteredItems = search.trim()
    ? group.items.filter((item) => item.label.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()))
    : group.items;

  return (
    <div
      className={cn(
        "flex h-full flex-col gap-4 border-r border-line bg-card py-4 transition-[width] duration-300 ease-out",
        collapsed ? "w-16 items-center px-0" : "w-72 px-3"
      )}
      style={{ transitionTimingFunction: softSpring }}
    >
      {showBrand && !collapsed && (
        <Link href="/instructor/dashboard" className="flex items-center gap-2 px-2 pb-2">
          <span className="font-display text-[16px] font-bold text-ink">
            Saachi<span className="text-gold-dark">AI</span>
          </span>
          <span className="rounded-full bg-paper-dim px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.06em] text-ink-soft">
            Instructor
          </span>
        </Link>
      )}

      {/* Section title + collapse toggle */}
      <div className={cn("flex items-center", collapsed ? "justify-center" : "justify-between px-1")}>
        {!collapsed && (
          <span className="font-display text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">{group.label}</span>
        )}
        <button
          type="button"
          aria-label={collapsed ? "Expand panel" : "Collapse panel"}
          onClick={() => setCollapsed((v) => !v)}
          className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink"
        >
          {collapsed ? <ChevronRight size={15} /> : <ChevronLeft size={15} />}
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Search */}
      {!collapsed && (
        <div className="flex h-10 items-center gap-2 rounded-control border border-line bg-paper-dim px-3">
          <Search size={14} className="shrink-0 text-ink-soft" />
          <input
            value={search}
            onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Search..."
            className="w-full bg-transparent text-[13px] text-ink placeholder:text-ink-soft/60 outline-none"
          />
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Items */}
      <nav className="flex flex-1 flex-col gap-1 overflow-y-auto">
        {filteredItems.map((item, idx) => {
          const itemKey = `${group.id}-${idx}`;
          const isExpanded = expanded.has(itemKey);
          const isActive = pathname === item.href || Boolean(pathname?.startsWith(item.href.split("?")[0] + "/"));

          return (
            <div key={itemKey} className="flex flex-col">
              {item.hasChildren ? (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => toggle(itemKey)}
                  title={collapsed ? item.label : undefined}
                  className={cn(
                    "flex items-center gap-2 rounded-control px-2.5 py-2 text-left text-[13px] font-medium transition-colors duration-150 ease-out",
                    collapsed ? "justify-center" : "justify-between",
                    isActive ? "bg-gold text-ink" : "text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink"
                  )}
                >
                  {!collapsed && <span className="truncate">{item.label}</span>}
                  {!collapsed && (
                    <ChevronDown
                      size={14}
                      className="shrink-0 transition-transform duration-200 ease-out"
                      style={{
                        transitionTimingFunction: softSpring,
                        transform: isExpanded ? "rotate(180deg)" : "rotate(0deg)"
                      }}
                    />
                  )}
                  {collapsed && <span className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-current" />}
                </button>
              ) : (
                <Link
                  href={item.href}
                  onClick={onNavigate}
                  title={collapsed ? item.label : undefined}
                  className={cn(
                    "flex items-center rounded-control px-2.5 py-2 text-[13px] font-medium transition-colors duration-150 ease-out",
                    collapsed ? "justify-center" : "justify-start",
                    isActive ? "bg-gold text-ink" : "text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink"
                  )}
                >
                  {collapsed ? <span className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-current" /> : item.label}
                </Link>
              )}

              {item.hasChildren && isExpanded && !collapsed && item.children && (
                <div className="ml-2 flex flex-col gap-0.5 border-l border-line pl-2 py-1">
                  {item.children.map((child, childIdx) => (
                    <Link
                      key={`${itemKey}-${childIdx}`}
                      href={child.href}
                      onClick={onNavigate}
                      className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-control px-2.5 py-1.5 text-[12.5px] text-ink-soft transition-colors duration-100 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink"
                    >
                      <span className="truncate">{child.label}</span>
                      {child.meta && (
                        <span className="ml-2 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-paper-dim px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold text-ink-soft">
                          {child.meta}
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </Link>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </nav>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ------------------------------ Combined (mobile) -------------------------- */

export function InstructorSidebarMobile({ onNavigate }: { onNavigate?: () => void }) {
  const [activeGroupId, setActiveGroupId] = useState("overview");
  const router = useRouter();

  function handleMobileClick(group: InstructorNavGroup) {
    setActiveGroupId(group.id);
    router.push(resolveGroupHref(group));
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex h-full">
      <div className="flex w-16 shrink-0 flex-col items-center gap-2 border-r border-line bg-card py-4">
        {INSTRUCTOR_SIDEBAR.map((group) => {
          const Icon = group.icon;
          const isActive = group.id === activeGroupId;
          return (
            <button
              key={group.id}
              type="button"
              title={group.label}
              onClick={() => handleMobileClick(group)}
              className={cn(
                "relative flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-control transition-colors duration-200 ease-out",
                isActive ? "bg-gold text-ink" : "text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink"
              )}
            >
              <Icon size={17} strokeWidth={1.75} />
              {group.badge && (
                <span className="absolute -right-1 -top-1 flex h-4 min-w-4 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-gold-dark px-1 text-[9px] font-semibold text-white">
                  {group.badge}
                </span>
              )}
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>
      <InstructorDetailPanel activeGroupId={activeGroupId} onNavigate={onNavigate} showBrand />
    </div>
  );
}

export default InstructorSidebarMobile;

FILE: apps\web\components\ui\learner-onboarding-checklist.tsx
-----------------------------------------------------------------
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { ChevronDown, X, CircleCheck, Circle } from "lucide-react";
import { useEnrollmentStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { Button, cn } from "@saachi/ui";

const DISMISS_KEY = "saachi-learner-onboarding-dismissed";

type ChecklistItem = {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  description: string;
  href: string;
  done: boolean;
};

export function LearnerOnboardingChecklist() {
  const enrollments = useEnrollmentStore((s) => s.enrollments);

  const [dismissed, setDismissed] = useState(true); // default true until we check localStorage (avoids flash)
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState(true);
  const [hydrated, setHydrated] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    setHydrated(true);
    const stored = window.localStorage.getItem(DISMISS_KEY);
    setDismissed(stored === "true");
  }, []);

  const hasEnrollment = enrollments.length > 0;
  const hasStartedLesson = enrollments.some((e) => e.progressPercent > 0);
  const hasCompletedCourse = enrollments.some((e) => e.progressPercent === 100);

  const items: ChecklistItem[] = [
    {
      id: "browse",
      label: "Browse the course marketplace",
      description: "Explore 1,280+ AI courses across automation, design, and development.",
      href: "/courses",
      done: true // reaching the dashboard implies they've seen the marketplace flow
    },
    {
      id: "enroll",
      label: "Enroll in your first course",
      description: "Pick a course that matches your goals and get started.",
      href: "/courses",
      done: hasEnrollment
    },
    {
      id: "lesson",
      label: "Start your first lesson",
      description: "Jump into a lesson and begin building real skills.",
      href: "/my-courses",
      done: hasStartedLesson
    },
    {
      id: "profile",
      label: "Complete your profile",
      description: "Add your details so instructors and the community know you.",
      href: "/settings",
      done: false
    },
    {
      id: "certificate",
      label: "Finish a course and earn a certificate",
      description: "Complete a course end-to-end to unlock your first certificate.",
      href: "/certificates",
      done: hasCompletedCourse
    }
  ];

  const completedCount = items.filter((i) => i.done).length;
  const allDone = completedCount === items.length;

  function handleDismiss() {
    window.localStorage.setItem(DISMISS_KEY, "true");
    setDismissed(true);
  }

  if (!hydrated) return null;
  if (dismissed) return null;

  return (
    <div className="mb-10 overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 px-5 py-4">
        <div className="flex-1">
          <div className="mb-1.5 flex items-center gap-2">
            <h2 className="text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">Get started on Saachi AI</h2>
            <span className="rounded-full bg-paper-dim px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-semibold text-ink-soft">
              {completedCount} of {items.length} complete
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="h-1.5 w-full max-w-xs overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-paper-dim">
            <div
              className="h-full rounded-full bg-gold transition-all duration-300 ease-out"
              style={{ width: `${(completedCount / items.length) * 100}%` }}
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setExpanded((v) => !v)}
          aria-label={expanded ? "Collapse checklist" : "Expand checklist"}
          className="flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink"
        >
          <ChevronDown
            size={16}
            className="transition-transform duration-200 ease-out"
            style={{ transform: expanded ? "rotate(180deg)" : "rotate(0deg)" }}
          />
        </button>

        {allDone && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleDismiss}
            aria-label="Dismiss checklist"
            className="flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink"
          >
            <X size={16} />
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      {expanded && (
        <div className="border-t border-line px-5 py-2">
          {items.map((item) => (
            <div
              key={item.id}
              className="flex items-center gap-3 border-b border-line py-3 last:border-0"
            >
              <span className={cn("shrink-0", item.done ? "text-teal" : "text-ink-soft/40")}>
                {item.done ? <CircleCheck size={18} /> : <Circle size={18} />}
              </span>
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <p
                  className={cn(
                    "text-[13.5px] font-semibold",
                    item.done ? "text-ink-soft line-through" : "text-ink"
                  )}
                >
                  {item.label}
                </p>
                <p className="text-[12px] text-ink-soft">{item.description}</p>
              </div>
              {!item.done && (
                <Link href={item.href} className="shrink-0">
                  <Button size="sm" variant="secondary">
                    Go
                  </Button>
                </Link>
              )}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export default LearnerOnboardingChecklist;



-----------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: apps\web\components\ui\learner-onboarding-tour.tsx
-----------------------------------------------------------------
"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from "framer-motion";
import * as Dialog from "@radix-ui/react-dialog";
import { X, Check, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, CheckCircle2, Circle } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, cn } from "@saachi/ui";

const SEEN_KEY = "saachi-learner-tour-seen";

export type TourStep = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  description?: string;
  targetSelector: string;
};

const STEPS: TourStep[] = [
  {
    id: "kpis",
    title: "Track your progress",
    description: "See your enrolled, completed, and unread notification counts at a glance.",
    targetSelector: "[data-onboard='kpi-row']"
  },
  {
    id: "continue",
    title: "Pick up where you left off",
    description: "Courses in progress show up here so you can jump straight back in.",
    targetSelector: "[data-onboard='continue-learning']"
  },
  {
    id: "nav",
    title: "Explore your courses",
    description: "Certificates, wishlist, orders, and settings are all in the sidebar.",
    targetSelector: "[data-onboard='nav-my-courses']"
  }
];

function getElementPosition(selector: string) {
  const element = document.querySelector(selector) as HTMLElement | null;
  if (!element) return null;
  const rect = element.getBoundingClientRect();
  return { top: rect.top + window.scrollY, left: rect.left + window.scrollX, width: rect.width, height: rect.height };
}

function Coachmark({
  step, stepIndex, totalSteps, isFirst, isLast, onNext, onPrev, onClose
}: {
  step: TourStep; stepIndex: number; totalSteps: number; isFirst: boolean; isLast: boolean;
  onNext: () => void; onPrev: () => void; onClose: () => void;
}) {
  const [pos, setPos] = useState(getElementPosition(step.targetSelector));

  const update = useCallback(() => setPos(getElementPosition(step.targetSelector)), [step.targetSelector]);

  useEffect(() => {
    update();
    window.addEventListener("resize", update);
    window.addEventListener("scroll", update);
    const target = document.querySelector(step.targetSelector);
    const ro = target ? new ResizeObserver(update) : null;
    if (target && ro) ro.observe(target);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("resize", update);
      window.removeEventListener("scroll", update);
      ro?.disconnect();
    };
  }, [step.targetSelector, update]);

  useEffect(() => {
    function onKey(e: KeyboardEvent) {
      if (e.key === "Escape") onClose();
      else if (e.key === "ArrowRight" && !isLast) onNext();
      else if (e.key === "ArrowLeft" && !isFirst) onPrev();
    }
    document.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, [onClose, onNext, onPrev, isFirst, isLast]);

  if (!pos) return null;
  const { top, left, width, height } = pos;
  const padding = 8;

  return (
    <motion.div
      initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
      animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
      exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 pointer-events-none"
      style={{
        background: `radial-gradient(circle at ${left + width / 2}px ${top + height / 2}px, transparent ${Math.max(width, height) / 2 + padding}px, rgba(0,0,0,0.65) ${Math.max(width, height) / 2 + padding + 1}px)`
      }}
    >
      <div
        className="absolute rounded-control border-2 border-gold shadow-lg"
        style={{ top: top - padding, left: left - padding, width: width + padding * 2, height: height + padding * 2 }}
      />
      <motion.div
        initial={{ scale: 0.9, opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
        animate={{ scale: 1, opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
        exit={{ scale: 0.9, opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
        transition={{ type: "spring", damping: 25, stiffness: 400 }}
        className="absolute w-80 max-w-[90vw] rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-4 shadow-xl pointer-events-auto"
        style={{
          top: Math.min(Math.max(16, top + height + 16), window.innerHeight - 200),
          left: Math.min(Math.max(16, left + width / 2 - 160), window.innerWidth - 336)
        }}
      >
        <p className="mb-1 text-[12px] font-semibold text-ink-soft">Step {stepIndex + 1} of {totalSteps}</p>
        <h3 className="mb-1 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">{step.title}</h3>
        {step.description && <p className="mb-4 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{step.description}</p>}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={onPrev} disabled={isFirst}>
            <ChevronLeft className="mr-1 h-4 w-4" /> Prev
          </Button>
          <Button size="sm" onClick={onNext}>
            {isLast ? "Finish" : "Next"}
            {!isLast && <ChevronRight className="ml-1 h-4 w-4" />}
          </Button>
        </div>
      </motion.div>
    </motion.div>
  );
}

export function LearnerOnboardingTour() {
  const [hydrated, setHydrated] = useState(false);
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [activeIndex, setActiveIndex] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    setHydrated(true);
    const seen = window.localStorage.getItem(SEEN_KEY) === "true";
    if (!seen) {
      const t = setTimeout(() => setOpen(true), 500); // let dashboard finish rendering first
      return () => clearTimeout(t);
    }
  }, []);

  function markSeen() {
    window.localStorage.setItem(SEEN_KEY, "true");
    setOpen(false);
    setActiveIndex(0);
  }

  if (!hydrated) return null;

  const step = STEPS[activeIndex];
  const isFirst = activeIndex === 0;
  const isLast = activeIndex === STEPS.length - 1;

  return (
    <>
      <Dialog.Root open={open} onOpenChange={(v) => { if (!v) markSeen(); }} modal={false}>
        <Dialog.Portal>
          <Dialog.Content
            className="fixed bottom-4 right-4 z-50 w-80 rounded-card border border-line bg-card shadow-xl pointer-events-auto"
            onOpenAutoFocus={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
            onPointerDownOutside={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
            onInteractOutside={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-line p-4">
              <Dialog.Title className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">Quick tour</Dialog.Title>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={markSeen}
                aria-label="Close tour"
                className="flex h-7 w-7 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim hover:text-ink"
              >
                <X size={15} />
              </button>
            </div>

            <ul className="space-y-1 p-3">
              {STEPS.map((s, i) => (
                <li key={s.id}>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setActiveIndex(i)}
                    className={cn(
                      "flex w-full items-center gap-2.5 rounded-control px-3 py-2 text-left text-[13px]",
                      i === activeIndex ? "bg-paper-dim text-ink" : "text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim"
                    )}
                  >
                    {i < activeIndex ? (
                      <CheckCircle2 size={16} className="shrink-0 text-teal" />
                    ) : (
                      <Circle size={16} className="shrink-0 text-ink-soft/40" />
                    )}
                    {s.title}
                  </button>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          </Dialog.Content>
        </Dialog.Portal>
      </Dialog.Root>

      <AnimatePresence>
        {open && step && (
          <Coachmark
            step={step}
            stepIndex={activeIndex}
            totalSteps={STEPS.length}
            isFirst={isFirst}
            isLast={isLast}
            onNext={() => (isLast ? markSeen() : setActiveIndex((i) => i + 1))}
            onPrev={() => setActiveIndex((i) => Math.max(0, i - 1))}
            onClose={markSeen}
          />
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>
    </>
  );
}

export default LearnerOnboardingTour;

"use client";

import * as React from "react";
import { PlusIcon, ShieldCheckIcon, TimerIcon } from "lucide-react";

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

// NOTE: demo-only offer window. Replace with a real campaign end-date
// from the backend once the promotions API is wired up.
const OFFER_DURATION_MS = 5 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000; // 5 days

type TimeLeft = {
  days: number;
  hours: number;
  minutes: number;
  seconds: number;
  expired: boolean;
};

function getTimeLeft(target: number): TimeLeft {
  const diff = target - Date.now();
  if (diff <= 0) {
    return { days: 0, hours: 0, minutes: 0, seconds: 0, expired: true };
  }
  const days = Math.floor(diff / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
  const hours = Math.floor((diff / (1000 * 60 * 60)) % 24);
  const minutes = Math.floor((diff / (1000 * 60)) % 60);
  const seconds = Math.floor((diff / 1000) % 60);
  return { days, hours, minutes, seconds, expired: false };
}

function pad(n: number) {
  return n.toString().padStart(2, "0");
}

function CountdownUnit({ value, label }: { value: number; label: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-center">
      <span className="font-mono text-[20px] font-bold leading-none text-ink md:text-[24px]">{pad(value)}</span>
      <span className="mt-1 text-[10px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-ink-soft">{label}</span>
    </div>
  );
}

const monthlyCtaClassName =
  "inline-flex h-11 w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-line-strong bg-card text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink transition-colors duration-150 ease-out hover:bg-paper-dim";

const yearlyCtaClassName =
  "inline-flex h-11 w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-full text-[15px] font-semibold text-white transition-transform duration-150 ease-out hover:brightness-110";

const yearlyCtaStyle = { backgroundImage: "var(--color-accent-gradient)" };

  React.useEffect(() => {
    const id = setInterval(() => {
      setTimeLeft(getTimeLeft(targetRef.current));
    }, 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(id);
  }, []);

  return (
    <section className="relative overflow-hidden py-10 md:py-16">
      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-6xl space-y-5 px-4">
        <motion.div
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
          whileInView={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          transition={{ duration: 0.8, delay: 0.1, ease: [0.16, 1, 0.3, 1] }}
          viewport={{ once: true }}

          </p>

          {!timeLeft.expired ? (

              <CountdownUnit value={timeLeft.days} label="Days" />

              <CountdownUnit value={timeLeft.hours} label="Hrs" />
              <span className="text-[20px] font-bold text-line-strong">:</span>
              <CountdownUnit value={timeLeft.minutes} label="Min" />
              <span className="text-[20px] font-bold text-line-strong">:</span>

              "bg-[size:32px_32px]",

            )}
          />

          <motion.div
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
            whileInView={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}

            viewport={{ once: true }}

          >
            <div className="relative grid border border-line bg-card p-4 md:grid-cols-2">
              <PlusIcon className="absolute -left-3 -top-3 size-5.5 text-ink-soft" />
              <PlusIcon className="absolute -right-3 -top-3 size-5.5 text-ink-soft" />
              <PlusIcon className="absolute -bottom-3 -left-3 size-5.5 text-ink-soft" />
              <PlusIcon className="absolute -bottom-3 -right-3 size-5.5 text-ink-soft" />

FILE: apps\web\components\ui\live-globe.tsx
-----------------------------------------------------------------
"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useCallback } from "react";
import createGlobe from "cobe";

export interface LiveMarker {
  id: string;
  location: [number, number];
}

interface GlobeLiveProps {
  markers?: LiveMarker[];
  className?: string;
  speed?: number;
}

const defaultMarkers: LiveMarker[] = [
  { id: "karachi", location: [24.86, 67.0] },
  { id: "london", location: [51.51, -0.13] },
  { id: "tokyo", location: [35.68, 139.65] },
  { id: "nyc", location: [40.71, -74.01] },
  { id: "sydney", location: [-33.87, 151.21] },
  { id: "lagos", location: [6.52, 3.38] }
];

export function GlobeLive({ markers = defaultMarkers, className = "", speed = 0.003 }: GlobeLiveProps) {
  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null);
  const pointerInteracting = useRef<{ x: number; y: number } | null>(null);
  const dragOffset = useRef({ phi: 0, theta: 0 });
  const phiOffsetRef = useRef(0);
  const thetaOffsetRef = useRef(0);
  const isPausedRef = useRef(false);
  const phiRef = useRef(0);

  const handlePointerDown = useCallback((e: React.PointerEvent) => {
    pointerInteracting.current = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };
    if (canvasRef.current) canvasRef.current.style.cursor = "grabbing";
    isPausedRef.current = true;
  }, []);

  const handlePointerUp = useCallback(() => {
    if (pointerInteracting.current !== null) {
      phiOffsetRef.current += dragOffset.current.phi;
      thetaOffsetRef.current += dragOffset.current.theta;
      dragOffset.current = { phi: 0, theta: 0 };
    }
    pointerInteracting.current = null;
    if (canvasRef.current) canvasRef.current.style.cursor = "grab";
    isPausedRef.current = false;
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handlePointerMove = (e: PointerEvent) => {
      if (pointerInteracting.current !== null) {
        dragOffset.current = {
          phi: (e.clientX - pointerInteracting.current.x) / 300,
          theta: (e.clientY - pointerInteracting.current.y) / 1000
        };
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("pointermove", handlePointerMove, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener("pointerup", handlePointerUp, { passive: true });
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("pointermove", handlePointerMove);
      window.removeEventListener("pointerup", handlePointerUp);
    };
  }, [handlePointerUp]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!canvasRef.current) return;
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    let globe: ReturnType<typeof createGlobe> | null = null;

    function init() {
      const size = canvas.offsetWidth;
      if (size === 0 || globe) return;

      const dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);

      // Render colors are WebGL RGB triples consumed directly by cobe's
      // renderer - not Tailwind utility classes - so they're literal values
      // here rather than CSS var references. Tuned to the brand accent
      // (accent-violet #7c3aed -> ~0.486, 0.227, 0.929).
      globe = createGlobe(canvas, {
        devicePixelRatio: dpr,
        width: size * dpr,
        height: size * dpr,
        phi: 0,
        theta: 0.2,
        dark: 0,
        diffuse: 1.5,
        mapSamples: 16000,
        mapBrightness: 10,
        baseColor: [0.93, 0.9, 0.97],
        markerColor: [0.486, 0.227, 0.929],
        glowColor: [0.94, 0.91, 0.98],
        markers: markers.map((m) => ({ location: m.location, size: 0.05 })),
        opacity: 0.85,
        onRender: (state) => {
          if (!isPausedRef.current) phiRef.current += speed;
          state.phi = phiRef.current + phiOffsetRef.current + dragOffset.current.phi;
          state.theta = 0.2 + thetaOffsetRef.current + dragOffset.current.theta;
          state.width = size * dpr;
          state.height = size * dpr;
        }
      });

      setTimeout(() => canvas && (canvas.style.opacity = "1"));
    }

    if (canvas.offsetWidth > 0) {
      init();
    } else {
      const ro = new ResizeObserver((entries) => {
        if (entries[0]?.contentRect.width > 0) {
          ro.disconnect();
          init();
        }
      });
      ro.observe(canvas);
    }

    return () => {
      if (globe) globe.destroy();
    };
  }, [markers, speed]);

  return (
    <div className={`relative aspect-square select-none ${className}`}>
      <canvas
        ref={canvasRef}
        onPointerDown={handlePointerDown}
        style={{
          width: "100%",
          height: "100%",
          cursor: "grab",
          opacity: 0,
          transition: "opacity 1.2s ease",
          borderRadius: "50%",
          touchAction: "none"
        }}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\components\ui\navbar-menu.tsx
-----------------------------------------------------------------
"use client";
import React from "react";
import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import Link from "next/link";
import Image from "next/image";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

const transition = {
  type: "spring",
  mass: 0.5,
  damping: 11.5,
  stiffness: 100,
  restDelta: 0.001,
  restSpeed: 0.001,
};

export const MenuItem = ({
  setActive,
  active,
  item,
  children,
}: {
  setActive: (item: string) => void;
  active: string | null;
  item: string;
  children?: React.ReactNode;
}) => {
  return (
    <div onMouseEnter={() => setActive(item)} className="relative">
      <motion.p
        transition={{ duration: 0.3 }}
        className="cursor-pointer text-[14px] font-medium text-ink-soft transition-colors hover:text-ink"
      >
        {item}
      </motion.p>
      {active !== null && (
        <motion.div
          initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.85, y: 10 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1, y: 0 }}
          transition={transition}
        >
          {active === item && (
            <div className="absolute top-[calc(100%_+_1.2rem)] left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 pt-4">
              <motion.div
                transition={transition}
                layoutId="active"
                className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-line bg-card shadow-xl backdrop-blur-sm"
              >
                <motion.div layout className="h-full w-max p-4">
                  {children}
                </motion.div>
              </motion.div>
            </div>
          )}
        </motion.div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export const Menu = ({
  setActive,
  children,
}: {
  setActive: (item: string | null) => void;
  children: React.ReactNode;
}) => {
  return (
    <nav
      onMouseLeave={() => setActive(null)}
      className="relative flex items-center justify-center gap-6"
    >
      {children}
    </nav>
  );
};

export const ProductItem = ({
  title,
  description,
  href,
  src,
}: {
  title: string;
  description: string;
  href: string;
  src: string;
}) => {
  return (
    <Link href={href} className="flex gap-3">
      <Image
        src={src}
        width={128}
        height={64}
        alt={title}
        className="h-16 w-32 shrink-0 rounded-md object-cover shadow-lg"
      />
      <div>
        <h4 className="mb-1 text-[14.5px] font-semibold text-ink">{title}</h4>
        <p className="max-w-[10rem] text-[12.5px] leading-snug text-ink-soft">{description}</p>
      </div>
    </Link>
  );
};

type HoveredLinkProps = React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<typeof Link> & {
  className?: string;
};

export const HoveredLink = ({ children, className, ...rest }: HoveredLinkProps) => {
  return (
    <Link
      {...rest}
      className={cn("text-[13.5px] text-ink-soft transition-colors hover:text-ink", className)}
    >
      {children}
    </Link>
  );
};

FILE: apps\web\components\ui\newsletter-2.tsx
-----------------------------------------------------------------
"use client";

import { RiArrowRightLine, RiLockLine } from "@remixicon/react";
import { toast } from "sonner";

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Checkbox } from "@/components/ui/checkbox";
import { Toaster } from "@/components/ui/sonner";

export default function NewsletterBlock() {
  return (
    <section className="flex w-full items-center justify-center rounded-card border border-line bg-card px-6 py-12 text-ink">
      <Toaster />
      <div className="w-full max-w-5xl px-2 py-2 sm:px-6 sm:py-4">

              Saachi AI Weekly
            </p>
            <h2 className="font-display text-2xl leading-tight font-bold tracking-tight text-ink sm:text-3xl">
              Get new courses in your inbox
            </h2>
            <p className="text-sm text-ink-soft">
              One email a week. New releases, instructor spotlights, and the
              occasional discount code.
            </p>

              className="flex flex-col gap-3"
              onSubmit={(e) => {

              }}

              <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 sm:flex-row">
                <input
                  type="email"
                  required
                  placeholder="you@example.com"
                  aria-label="Email address"
                  className="h-10 flex-1 rounded-control border border-line-strong bg-card px-3.5 text-[14px] text-ink outline-none transition-colors duration-150 ease-out placeholder:text-ink-soft/50 focus:border-gold"
                />
                <Button
                  type="submit"
                  className="shrink-0 rounded-full px-5 text-white shadow-[0_0_24px_-8px_rgba(124,58,237,0.55)] hover:brightness-110"
                  style={{ backgroundImage: "var(--color-accent-gradient)", border: "none" }}
                >
                  Subscribe
                  <RiArrowRightLine aria-hidden="true" className="ml-1.5" size={16} />
                </Button>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Checkbox id="newsletter-consent" name="consent" />
                <label
                  htmlFor="newsletter-consent"
                  className="text-sm font-normal text-ink-soft"
                >
                  I agree to receive the Saachi AI Weekly newsletter.
                </label>
              </div>

            <p className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-ink-soft">
              <RiLockLine aria-hidden="true" className="shrink-0" size={12} />
              Your data stays private. Unsubscribe any time, no questions asked.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );

FILE: apps\web\components\ui\pricing-tiers.tsx
-----------------------------------------------------------------
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Check } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

export type PricingPlan = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  price: string;
  frequency?: string;
  description: string;
  features: string[];
  buttonText: string;
  buttonLink: string;
  isFeatured?: boolean;
  badge?: string;
};

export type PricingSectionData = {
  title: string;
  subtitle?: string;
  plans: PricingPlan[];
};

function PricingTier({
  name,
  price,
  frequency,
  description,
  features,
  buttonText,
  buttonLink,
  isFeatured = false,
  badge
}: PricingPlan) {
  return (
    <div
      className={cn(
        "flex w-full max-w-sm flex-col rounded-card border p-8 transition-transform duration-200",
        isFeatured
          ? "border-accent-violet/50 bg-gold-tint shadow-xl ring-1 ring-accent-violet/30 md:-translate-y-2"
          : "border-line bg-card shadow-sm hover:-translate-y-1"
      )}
    >
      {isFeatured && badge && (
        <span className="mb-3 w-fit self-center rounded-full bg-gold-dark px-3 py-1 text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-white">
          {badge}
        </span>
      )}

      <h3 className="text-center font-display text-[22px] font-bold text-ink">{name}</h3>

      <div className="mt-4 flex items-baseline justify-center gap-1">
        <span className="font-mono text-[42px] font-extrabold leading-none text-ink">{price}</span>
        {frequency && <span className="text-[14px] font-medium text-ink-soft">{frequency}</span>}
      </div>

      <p className="mt-4 text-center text-[13.5px] leading-relaxed text-ink-soft">{description}</p>

      <ul className="mt-8 flex flex-1 flex-col gap-3.5">
        {features.map((feature) => (
          <li key={feature} className="flex items-start gap-3 text-[13.5px] text-ink">
            <span
              className={cn(
                "mt-0.5 flex h-5 w-5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full",
                isFeatured ? "bg-accent-violet text-white" : "bg-teal-light text-teal"
              )}
            >
              <Check size={12} strokeWidth={3} />
            </span>
            {feature}
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>

      <Link
        href={buttonLink}
        className={cn(
          "mt-8 w-full rounded-full py-3 text-center text-[13.5px] font-semibold transition-transform hover:scale-[1.02]",
          isFeatured ? "bg-ink text-paper" : "border border-line text-ink hover:bg-paper-dim"
        )}
      >
        {buttonText}
      </Link>
    </div>
  );
}

export function PricingSection({
  data,
  className,
  header
}: {
  data: PricingSectionData;
  className?: string;
  header?: ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <div className={className}>
      {header ?? (
        <div className="mb-10 text-center">
          <h2 className="font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink md:text-[32px]">{data.title}</h2>
          {data.subtitle && (
            <p className="mx-auto mt-3 max-w-xl text-[14.5px] text-ink-soft">{data.subtitle}</p>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
      <div className="flex flex-col items-stretch justify-center gap-6 md:flex-row md:items-center">
        {data.plans.map((plan) => (
          <PricingTier key={plan.id} {...plan} />
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default PricingSection;

FILE: apps\web\components\ui\search-command.tsx
-----------------------------------------------------------------
"use client";

/**
 * SearchCommand
 * cmdk-based command palette that powers the TopSearchBar dropdown.
 * Keyboard-navigable, grouped by content type, zero custom CSS hacks.
 */
import { useEffect, useRef, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { Command } from "cmdk";
import Link from "next/link";
import {
  GraduationCap,
  Newspaper,
  BookOpen,
  Users,
  Award,
  ArrowUpRight,
  Search
} from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

export type SearchGroup = {
  key: string;
  label: string;
  icon: ReactNode;
  items: { id: string; title: string; href: string; sub?: string }[];
};

type SearchCommandProps = {
  query: string;
  groups: SearchGroup[];
  onClose: () => void;
  className?: string;
};

export function SearchCommand({ query, groups, onClose, className }: SearchCommandProps) {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  // close on outside click
  useEffect(() => {
    function handleClick(e: MouseEvent) {
      if (ref.current && !ref.current.contains(e.target as Node)) onClose();
    }
    document.addEventListener("mousedown", handleClick);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("mousedown", handleClick);
  }, [onClose]);

  const totalItems = groups.reduce((s, g) => s + g.items.length, 0);
  const isEmpty = totalItems === 0;

  return (
    <div
      ref={ref}
      className={cn(
        "absolute left-0 right-0 top-[calc(100%+8px)] z-50 overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-line bg-card shadow-2xl",
        className
      )}
    >
      <Command className="w-full" shouldFilter={false}>
        <Command.List className="max-h-[420px] overflow-y-auto p-1.5">
          {isEmpty ? (
            <Command.Empty className="py-10 text-center text-[13px] text-ink-soft">
              {query.trim()
                ? `No results for "${query}"`
                : "Start typing to search across courses, news, blog, and more."}
            </Command.Empty>
          ) : (
            groups.map((group) => {
              if (group.items.length === 0) return null;
              return (
                <Command.Group
                  key={group.key}
                  heading={
                    <div className="mb-1 flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2 pt-2 pb-0.5 text-[10.5px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-ink-soft/60">
                      {group.icon}
                      {group.label}
                    </div>
                  }
                >
                  {group.items.map((item) => (
                    <Command.Item
                      key={item.id}
                      value={item.id}
                      asChild
                      className="cursor-pointer rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-[13px] transition-colors aria-selected:bg-gold-tint aria-selected:text-ink"
                      onSelect={() => onClose()}
                    >
                      <Link
                        href={item.href}
                        onClick={onClose}
                        className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 outline-none"
                      >
                        <div className="min-w-0">
                          <p className="truncate font-medium text-ink">{item.title}</p>
                          {item.sub && (
                            <p className="mt-0.5 truncate text-[11.5px] text-ink-soft">{item.sub}</p>
                          )}
                        </div>
                        <ArrowUpRight size={13} className="shrink-0 text-ink-soft/60" />
                      </Link>
                    </Command.Item>
                  ))}
                </Command.Group>
              );
            })
          )}

          {/* footer: see all */}
          {query.trim() && (
            <div className="border-t border-line mt-1 pt-1">
              <Command.Item
                value="__see_all__"
                asChild
                className="cursor-pointer rounded-lg aria-selected:bg-gold-tint"
                onSelect={onClose}
              >
                <Link
                  href={`/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(query)}`}
                  onClick={onClose}
                  className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-2.5 text-[12.5px] font-semibold text-accent-violet outline-none hover:underline"
                >
                  <Search size={13} />
                  See all results for &quot;{query}&quot;
                </Link>
              </Command.Item>
            </div>
          )}
        </Command.List>
      </Command>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\components\ui\separator.tsx

"use client"

import * as React from "react"
import * as SeparatorPrimitive from "@radix-ui/react-separator"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

type SeparatorProps = React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<typeof SeparatorPrimitive.Root>

const Separator = React.forwardRef<React.ElementRef<typeof SeparatorPrimitive.Root>, SeparatorProps>(
  function Separator({ className, orientation = "horizontal", decorative = true, ...props }, ref) {
    return (
      <SeparatorPrimitive.Root
        ref={ref}
        decorative={decorative}
        orientation={orientation}
        className={cn(
          "shrink-0 bg-line",
          orientation === "horizontal" ? "h-[1px] w-full" : "h-full w-[1px]",
          className
        )}
        {...props}
      />
    )
  }
)

export { Separator }

"use client"

import { useTheme } from "next-themes"
import { Toaster as Sonner } from "sonner"

type ToasterProps = React.ComponentProps<typeof Sonner>

const Toaster = ({ ...props }: ToasterProps) => {
  const { theme = "system" } = useTheme()

  return (
    <Sonner
      theme={theme as ToasterProps["theme"]}
      className="toaster group"
      toastOptions={{
        classNames: {
          toast:
            "group toast group-[.toaster]:bg-background group-[.toaster]:text-foreground group-[.toaster]:border-border group-[.toaster]:shadow-lg",
          description: "group-[.toast]:text-muted-foreground",
          actionButton:
            "group-[.toast]:bg-primary group-[.toast]:text-primary-foreground",
          cancelButton:
            "group-[.toast]:bg-muted group-[.toast]:text-muted-foreground",
        },
      }}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

export { Toaster }

export type CategoryDef = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  slug: string;
};

export const CATEGORIES: CategoryDef[] = [
  { id: "ai-fundamentals", name: "AI Fundamentals & Prompt Engineering", slug: "ai-fundamentals" },
  { id: "ai-marketing", name: "AI for Marketing & Sales", slug: "ai-marketing" },
  { id: "ai-automation", name: "AI Automation & Workflow Tools", slug: "ai-automation" },
  { id: "ai-support", name: "AI Customer Support & Communication", slug: "ai-support" },
  { id: "ai-dev", name: "AI Website & App Development", slug: "ai-dev" },
  { id: "ai-integration", name: "AI Integration for Developers", slug: "ai-integration" },
  { id: "ai-design", name: "AI Design & Creative Tools", slug: "ai-design" },
  { id: "ai-career", name: "AI for Careers & Job Search", slug: "ai-career" }
];

export function getCategoryBySlug(slug: string): CategoryDef | undefined {
  return CATEGORIES.find((c) => c.slug === slug);
}

import { clsx, type ClassValue } from "clsx";
import { twMerge } from "tailwind-merge";

export function cn(...inputs: ClassValue[]) {
  return twMerge(clsx(inputs));
}

import type { Course, CourseDetail, Session } from "./types";

function img(seed: string, w: number, h: number): string {
  return `https://picsum.photos/seed/${encodeURIComponent(seed)}/${w}/${h}`;
}

export const DUMMY_SESSION: Session = {

  email: "demo@saachi.ai",
  role: "learner",
  avatarUrl: img("ayesha-khan-demo", 150, 150)
};

export type DummyCourse = CourseDetail & {
  categoryId: string;
  level: "beginner" | "intermediate" | "advanced";
  language: "en" | "ur";
};

function toCourseSummary(course: DummyCourse): Course {
  return {
    id: course.id,
    slug: course.slug,
    title: course.title,
    subtitle: course.subtitle,
    thumbnailUrl: course.thumbnailUrl,
    price: course.price,
    currency: course.currency,
    rating: course.rating,
    ratingCount: course.ratingCount,
    instructor: course.instructor,
    status: course.status
  };
}

export const DUMMY_COURSES: DummyCourse[] = BASE_COURSES.map((course, index) => {
  const others = BASE_COURSES.filter((c) => c.id !== course.id);
  const related = [others[index % others.length], others[(index + 1) % others.length]].filter(Boolean);
  return { ...course, relatedCourses: related.map(toCourseSummary) };
});
